Static diff exact: `hypot` is declared through a special-case branch (**E-288**), `log1p`/`expm1` nowhere. **Also**: a 32-link `localparam` chain was refused in a compile-time table as *not a compile-time constant* -- the fold bound counts PARAMETER HOPS and stopped at 32, while the same chain as a plain value worked at any depth and nested arithmetic was unaffected -- now **512** in both folders, which must accept the same set (**E-508**); and a folded `atanh` disagreed with run time for a NEGATIVE argument near -1 (**1.3e-10** at -0.9999999, growing toward the boundary, run time exact) -- atanh is ODD, so the negative side folds through the accurate positive side. **RECORDED, NOT FIXED**: folding loses the SIGN OF ZERO (`1.0/ceil(-0.5)` +inf folded vs -inf at run time); rewriting `Fneg` as `0-x` was tried and REVERTED -- a constant operand never reaches that arm, so it fixed nothing and only removed an optimisation, and shipping it would have looked like a fix without being one. **WITHDRAWN at fix time**: a run-time out-of-range array index reading element 0 is documented at the lowering site as *DELIBERATE and load-bearing* (the select chain exists so it can never read out of bounds), with NaN and splitting parameter-from-variable both explicitly rejected there. Verified `lrmintrin` **21/21** both solvers (on the shipped binaries only **13 checks can run**, 4 pass), regression **424/424**. |
| [E-512](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-512.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, examples/transedge_examples/ (new), examples/rtdomain_examples/verify_rtdomain.py, examples/hiername_examples/verify_hiername.py | **`transition` REACHED ITS FINAL VALUE ONLY FOR SLOW EDGES.** User-reported, from reading the compliance document's *documented deviation* and asking whether the operators are actually correct. `transition`/`slew` are one rate-limited tracking loop `dy/dt = clamp(K*(x-y), -1/tfall, +1/trise)`; while the clamp is saturated that is an exact linear ramp at the LRM's rate, and it releases once the remaining gap falls below `rate/K`, leaving a first-order tail with tau = 1/K. **K was a FIXED 1e9/s**, so the gap was `1/(K*trise)`: at trise=3ns only **66.7%** of the swing was linear and the output reached **0.8774** where the LRM requires 1.0 (96.7%/0.9873 at 30ns, ~100%/1.000039 at 3us). The shortfall is `e^-1/(K*trise)` -- **0.877382 measured against 0.877374 predicted** -- which is what identified the mechanism. **Not a timestep artifact**: refining 100x converged to 0.8776, the WRONG value. **FIX** `K = TRACK_C * rate`, gap `1/TRACK_C` at every speed; **TRACK_C=1e3 BY MEASUREMENT** because the gap also bounds truncation error at the ramp's corner, so **E-47**'s plateau goes 0.875 -> 0.874940 -> 0.874766 at 1e3/1e4/1e5 -- bigger is WORSE. **PRESERVED**: an instantaneous edge (**E-504** clamps a negative rise to 0, reciprocal +inf, and `inf*0.0` is NaN) keeps the old fixed gain; and one gain from the FASTER rate, since a per-direction gain makes the loop jump at the crossing point (`transition(x,td,0.5n,-0.5n)` overshot to 1.01). **WHY IT SURVIVED**: `defaulttransition` pins the 1us case, deep inside the correct region -- the same blind spot as **E-510**'s literal folding. **Two suites adjusted, assertions intact**: `rtdomain` [5] required equal LENGTHS before it could report a difference, and a properly rate-limited ramp needs more timepoints (119 vs 102), so it reported *the same* for waveforms that had become MORE different; `hiername` asks for a 1ns step on a 1ns edge (it tests hierarchical names, not shape) and its 1e-6 tolerance was pinning integration noise on a level that is physically 1.0 -- exact again once the deck resolves its own edge. Verified `transedge` **14/14** both solvers (**4 fail pre-fix**, measured 10/14), regression **426/426**. |
| [E-514](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-514.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/builtin.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation/body.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation.rs, examples/lrmops_examples/ (new) | **The analog operators audited clause-by-clause against Accellera VAMS-2023 4.5.** `slew`/`transition` restarted every transient from 0: the tracking loop's **reactive** residual was gated on `enable_integration` and forced to `0.0` at DC, so the charge state ngspice seeds the transient from held 0 while the operating point had solved `y = x` -- the first step then saw `dy/dt = (y-0)/h`, the clamp bounded it, and the output ramped at exactly the slew rate for the bias divided by the rate seconds. LRM 4.5.9 says `slew` *returns the value of expr* when the input is not moving. The DC solution is unaffected (`CALC_REACT_JACOBIAN` is clear there), and E-47's reason for switching the RESISTIVE residual does not apply to the reactive one. Also: `transition`'s `time_tol = 0` is DEFINED by 4.5.8 and was refused; `idtmod(...,nature)` (Table 4-19) declared its last argument as a real, making it byte-identical to the `abstol` form so it could never match; a negative `zi_*` transition time (4.5.12 *shall be nonnegative*) compiled in silence; a zero-transition z-filter contributed directly to a branch now warns; and a constant `td` exceeding `maxdelay` is a warning, not an error, because 4.5.7 defines a substitution. Three existing suites were updated to match, each recorded in the write-up -- most notably `defaulttransition`, whose `dt_bare`/`dt_forms` flipped their input with `@(timer(0, 4u))`, which fires DURING the DC solve, so the operating point already held the flipped value and the old ramp existed only because the state contradicted it. |
| [E-515](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-515.md) | openvaf/preprocessor/src/{lib,parser,processor,diagnostics}.rs, openvaf/basedb/src/lib.rs, openvaf/basedb/src/diagnostics/{preprocessor_error,syntax_error}.rs, openvaf/hir/src/elaborate.rs, openvaf/lexer/src/lib.rs, openvaf/tokens/src/{lexer,lib}.rs, openvaf/parser/src/grammar/expressions.rs, openvaf/syntax/src/{name,parsing,validation,error,ast}.rs, sourcegen/src/ast.rs, examples/lrmlex_examples/ (new) | **THE LEXICAL LAYER AND COMPILER DIRECTIVES, AUDITED AGAINST LRM CLAUSES 2 AND 10.** **TWO THIRDS OF A BASED LITERAL WERE UNWRITABLE**: 2.6.1 makes a based constant 'up to three tokens', the digits 'optionally preceded by white space', and says 'It shall be legal to macro substitute these three tokens' -- its own Example 2 writes `5 'D 3` -- yet only the contiguous spelling compiled; the spaced forms died at the apostrophe and a macro-substituted size (`` `SZ'hFF ``) could not be written at all. Two new token kinds (a complete unsized literal, a bare base prefix), a literal-joining grammar rule, and joined-text evaluation share the semantics with the contiguous form byte for byte; a joined literal whose digits are illegal for its base is a located error, because the multi-token form would otherwise degrade to a SILENT ZERO. **THE PREDEFINED-MACRO SURFACE**: `__VAMS_ENABLE__` 'shall always be defined' (10.5) and was not defined at all -- the LRM's own not_gate idiom could never take its VAMS branch; `` `undef `` 'shall have no effect on predefined Verilog-AMS macros' (10.4) and removed them; a user macro in the reserved `__VAMS_` namespace now warns. **THREE WORDS RESERVED THAT ANNEX B NEVER RESERVED**: `real assert;` was a hard error, `root`/`do` unexpected tokens. `assert` left the reservation list; `root` became the keyword table's first CONTEXTUAL entry (ROOT_KW survives for the `$root` spelling); `do` is reclassified by one token of lookahead, so `real do;` compiles while the do-while extension still parses. **EXPRESSION-POSITION ATTRIBUTES** (2.9: 'as a suffix to an operator or a Verilog-AMS function name') parse now, after binary/unary operators, after `?`, and before an argument list; the audit's first-wins duplicate-attribute finding was REFUTED -- the reversed iterators already implement 'the last attribute value shall be used', and the suite pins it through the descriptor text. **DIRECTIVES**: `` `begin_keywords ``/`` `end_keywords `` exist (five specifiers validated, 1364-* warns the set is not narrowed, a stray end warns); `` `resetall `` resets the tracked directive state instead of warning 'unsupported' -- making the compliance document's claim true; and using `` `__FILE__``/`` `__LINE__ `` no longer breaks every RELATIVE `` `include `` in the compilation (the srcloc rewrite lost the working directory; the original file's parent now leads the synthetic root's include path). **ILLEGAL FORMS THAT COMPILED IN SILENCE**: `1.` (2.6.2 lists `9.` as illegal) and a string spanning a raw newline (2.7: 'contained on a single line') are located errors. Verified `lrmlex` **26/26** both solvers, regression clean across the committed suites. |
| [E-516](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-516.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/{callbacks,ctx,expr,fmt,stmt}.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/inference.rs, openvaf/osdi/stdlib.c, openvaf/osdi/src/compilation_unit.rs, openvaf/osdi/header/osdi_0_4.h, examples/lrmsysio_examples/ (new) | **DISPLAY AND FILE I/O, DEFERRED TO THE ACCEPTED ITERATION.** LRM 9.4.6 is one sentence -- 'All the display tasks, except $debug, shall not display output unless an iteration has been accepted' -- and 9.5.9 is its file twin. Neither held: a single .op of a diode printed **FIFTEEN** $strobe lines walking the unconverged Newton iterates, and an un-gated $fdisplay wrote five lines, the first holding **v=0**, a solution the circuit never settled at. Output now buffers per iteration and flushes when the point is ACCEPTED; statements inside event-controlled blocks carry a new LOG_FLAG_IMMEDIATE bit because they fire on the event's OWN iteration -- deferring @(initial_step) logging would have DROPPED it, and the events suites pin that it still prints exactly once. **$monitor HAD NO CHANGE DETECTION** (9.4.1: printed only 'if a variable or expression in the argument list changes value compared with the last accepted step'): 120 lines over a transient whose watched value changed ONCE. It lowers with its own level now and the simulator compares each accepted point's k-th monitor text with the previous point's: one line per change (a $abstime argument defeats the text comparison -- documented). **%r PRINTED GARBAGE FOR EVERY VALUE** (9.4.3): the scale index used the NATURAL log, never applied the table offset, and the codegen handed snprintf's %c the GEP POINTER into the scale table. `1e3 -> 1.000000k`, `1e-9 -> 1.000000n`, `0.036 -> 36.000000m` now, with 0/NaN/inf pinned to the unit scale. **THE FILE LIFECYCLE**: a 'w' reopen in a following analysis APPENDS (9.5.1.1 -- it truncated, so only the last analysis survived); the open-write-close idiom WROTE NOTHING (hoisted init code closed the descriptor before eval's first write -- an instance-setup $fclose now defers to the first accepted flush); ngspice re-runs instance initialization for setup AND temperature, so a re-run now freopens fresh and a $rewind/$fseek file comes out byte-exact ('XY234**789', not 'XY234**0123456789'); readable streams rewind to their accepted baseline each iteration (the read replay of 9.5.9) while write-only streams never rewind; and the pre-opened descriptors of 9.5.1 reach the standard streams, with the per-target symbol names baked into each triple's stdlib bitcode. A null display argument -- two adjacent commas -- prints ONE SPACE instead of failing to compile. **TWO DESIGNS THE REPO'S OWN SUITES REVERTED**, kept in the write-up because the mechanism matters: un-hoisting file ops via op-dependence tainted every descriptor use and broke `fileio`'s parameter-table report (the CLOSE TIMING was the bug, not the hoisting); and weak-ODR on every stdlib FUNCTION broke `%o`/`%b` scanning through LLVM's per-unit specialized clones -- the mutable STATE is weak now, one instance per .osdi, functions stay internal. Verified `lrmsysio` **19/19**, `display` **18/18**, `fileio` **9/9**, `scanfmt` **31/31**, all both solvers; events/mfactor/noise suites unchanged; full cargo test green (five MIR snapshots renumbered). |
| [E-517](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-517.md) | openvaf/hir/src/rec_declarations.rs, openvaf/hir/src/elaborate.rs, openvaf/sim_back/src/module_info.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{inference,diagnostics,types}.rs, openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, openvaf/syntax/src/{validation.rs,ast/expr_ext.rs}, openvaf/basedb/src/diagnostics/syntax_error.rs, examples/lrmdata_examples/ (new) | **DATA TYPES AND PARAMETERS, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 3.1-3.5.** A `(* desc *)` variable inside a named block was exported as an OSDI operating-point variable under its bare name -- 3.2.1 says block-level units/descriptions 'shall be ignored by the simulator' -- and two blocks declaring the same name produced DUPLICATE opvars; the intended filter compared `to_path(name)` against the bare name, but the declaration walker never pushes block names, so the two were always equal and the filter was dead. A string LITERAL now converts to an integral type per 3.3 (`'A'`=65, `'AB'`=0x4142, `'ABCDE'` truncated on the left) where it was a hard error -- on BOTH the assignment and declaration-initializer paths, which type-check through different code (`infere_assignment` never consults the `satisfies` table; the first fix landed only in the latter and the suite caught it). A string VALUE stays refused. **Whole-array overrides at instantiation** (3.4.4/3.4.8): `#(.cf('{9,8,7}))` distributes to the per-element parameters, 1-D and multi-dimensional, with 'the sizes shall match' enforced -- the LRM's own crosstalk example form, previously 'names no parameter'. **Alias in module equations** (3.4.7 'shall not occur anywhere else in the module') is a targeted compile error; `\$param_given` through the alias (E-59) still works. **And the battery caught a shipping regression**: BSIM4 stopped compiling under E-515's single-line-string rule -- it splits long `\$strobe` messages with backslash-newline. That pair is a LINE CONTINUATION now, contributing nothing to the string (the old unescaper KEPT the newline, which would cut each message mid-sentence; every one ends with its own explicit `\n`); a bare newline stays the E-515 error. Verified `lrmdata` **20/20** both solvers. |
| [E-518](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-518.md) | openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation/body.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{validation,inference,diagnostics}.rs, openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, openvaf/mir_llvm/src/builder.rs, openvaf/mir_opt/src/{const_eval,simplify}.rs, openvaf/{lexer,tokens,parser,syntax}/ (===/!== tokens), sourcegen/src/ast/src.rs, openvaf/basedb/src/diagnostics/sink.rs, examples/lrmexpr_examples/ (new) | **EXPRESSIONS AND MATH, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 4.1-4.4.** Table 4-16 lists `V(n1,n1)`/`I(n1,n1)` as ERROR; `V(a,a)` compiled with NO diagnostic and silently read 0. Both are located errors now, with two deliberate boundaries: a named degenerate branch DECLARATION keeps its E-414 warning, and accesses inside an ELABORATION BUFFER keep the old defined semantics -- a flattened instantiation legally ties two terminals to one node (a diode-connected transistor; the LRM's own ECP oscillator), and the first build broke `lrm_examples` on exactly that model. A latent bug found next door: the nature-access validator read `args[0]` TWICE, so its incompatible-discipline arm could never fire. **`%` by a deck-supplied zero** (4.2.4 'it shall be an error') produced NaN and a generic convergence failure; it aborts with `OSDI(fatal) ... LRM 4.2.4` now via the E-509 three-route policy, both integer and real paths (first cut printed garbage -- the message's leading `%` was eaten by the printf-style formatter). **Integer division was genuine UB**: raw LLVM `sdiv`/`srem` -- poison for the optimizer, SIGFPE on the x86 builds this project ships -- the moment a runtime divisor crossed zero. Pinned at the lowering per the E-335 precedent: `x/0`=0, `INT_MIN/-1` wraps, `x%0`=0; the MIR constant folder folds to the same values, so folded chains and compiled models agree. **A literal shift distance outside 0..=31 was a hard error while the runtime path already computed the LRM's answer** (4.2.11: the distance is unsigned, `1<<32` IS 0): now a warning plus the correct value, both paths agreeing (`-8>>>34` = sign fill). **`===`/`!==` lex** (new EQ3/NEQ2 tokens through sourcegen) at the `==` precedence and evaluate as 2-state `==`/`!=` -- exact where no x/z bits exist; they died as `==` then a stray `=`. **`<<<`/`>>>` are a flagged extension** (4.2.11 bars them from analog blocks; `>>>` is the only sign-extending spelling): each use warns, and all thirteen big CMC models compile with zero warnings. Verified `lrmexpr` **22/22** both solvers. |
| [E-519](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-519.md) | openvaf/hir_ty/src/lower.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation/types.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation.rs, openvaf/syntax/src/validation.rs, openvaf/osdi/src/ndatable.rs, openvaf/vfs/src/va_std.rs, examples/lrmdisc_examples/ (new, incl. dump_nda.c dlopen harness) | **NATURES, DISCIPLINES AND BRANCHES, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 3.6-3.13 AND ANNEX D.** `DisciplineTy::compatible` required every nature binding both-present-or-both-absent, rejecting what 3.11.1 declares compatible -- the LRM's own worked example says electrical and sig_flow_v ARE compatible ('the nature for flow does not exist in sig_flow_v'), and the stock signal-flow discipline `voltage` IS sig_flow_v -- plus every natureless and domainless connection. All six rules implemented, with effective-domain resolution per 3.6.2.2 (natures default to continuous; no natures + no domain = domainless, compatible with everything). One consequence: compatible disciplines are no longer interchangeable, so the BRANCH takes the discipline that HAS the natures -- `I(br)` across electrical/voltage works, verified as a live 1k element -- where the picker used to take the first node's arbitrarily. **The nature-attribute checks were DEAD CODE**: `validate_nature_decl` iterated `attrs()` -- the `(* ... *)` ANNOTATION accessor, which a nature declaration never has -- so the written checks visited zero items and `access = "SA"` compiled clean, dying later as 'SA was not found in the current scope' after lowering silently dropped the value. One word (`nature_attrs()`) makes them live. Two more 3.6.1.2 rules WARN: units on a derived nature (the declared value is silently ignored -- 'always inherits its parent nature units' -- so `"furlong"` staying `"V"` deserved a voice) and an idt/ddt override unrelated to the parent's link. **Every OSDI nature descriptor over-counted its attributes** (`attr_vec.len() + 1`; the discipline path right below has no +1): a consumer walking `attr_start..+num_attr` read the NEXT nature's first attribute -- latent only because ngspice never reads the table; pinned by a committed dlopen harness asserting every range exact and contiguous. **constants.vams is the VAMS-2023 Annex D.2 file**: `PHYSICAL_CONSTANTS_NIST2018` selects the exact-SI values (P_Q 1.602176634e-19, P_U0 1.25663706212e-6) where the opt-in silently produced NIST1998 numbers; the default stays NIST1998 exactly as the 2023 LRM specifies. E-39's derived-access extension and E-422's optional-attribute decision STAND (both were implemented once and withdrawn under their own suites) and are recorded in the compliance doc; vector branches and out-of-module discipline declarations are documented as unsupported. Verified `lrmdisc` **21/21** both solvers. |
| [E-520](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-520.md) | openvaf/syntax/veriloga.ungram, sourcegen/src/ast/src.rs, openvaf/{lexer,tokens,parser}/ (BREAK/CONTINUE/RETURN), openvaf/syntax/src/{parsing,validation}.rs, openvaf/hir_def/src/{expr.rs,body/lower.rs,body/pretty.rs}, openvaf/hir/src/body.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{inference.rs,validation/body.rs,validation.rs}, openvaf/hir_lower/src/{ctx,stmt,expr}.rs, openvaf/preprocessor/src/{processor,diagnostics,tests}.rs, openvaf/basedb/src/diagnostics/preprocessor_error.rs, examples/lrmjump_examples/ (new) | **JUMP STATEMENTS, STRING FUNCTIONS, AND THE ANNEX C/E BOUNDARY.** VAMS-2023 5.11 provides `break`/`continue`/`return` in the analog grammar; NONE existed ('break was not found in the current scope'), and the compliance doc claimed the LANGUAGE has no such keywords, citing a disable-idiom diagnostic that never existed. Implemented end to end: one new `JumpStmt` grammar node; the words are CONTEXTUAL keywords (keyword only in statement shape -- boundary token before, `;`/expression start after -- so legacy identifiers named `break` still compile, flagged by L012); `return expr` types exactly like an assignment to the function-name variable. Lowering reuses the `disable` shape: loops carry (continue, break) target blocks -- `for` gained a dedicated INCREMENT block so `continue` still increments, `repeat` a LATCH block so `continue` still counts -- loop exits seal only after the body (a `break` adds a predecessor), and every inlined function body gets an exit block `return` jumps to after optionally writing the return place. The 5.9.3 exclusion (no jumps in a genvar analog_for) costs ZERO code: those loops unroll at elaboration, so a jump inside one reaches validation loop-less and draws the outside-a-loop error. All semantics pinned numerically, including return-from-a-loop-inside-a-function. **A string-typed analog function CRASHED the compiler** (4.7.1, legal since Mantis 7808): two `unreachable!` arms in the function inliner; both now initialize with `""` per 4.7.2.1/4.7.2.3 -- string returns and string OUTPUT arguments work end to end. **\$realtime in the analog context** draws the Table 9-7 deprecation warning (it behaves as \$abstime; VAMS 2.0-2.4 scaled to `timescale` units, so legacy source reads 1e9 off), and **`default_discipline`** warns as an ignored AMS-only directive (C.4) instead of being silently swallowed. Doc corrections: casex/casez and the legacy analog `generate` are marked as deliberate extensions beyond Annex C (C.7, G.2.3); the stale lrm_examples counts now quote manifest.json (47/12/21); the Annex E SPICE-primitive boundary is documented for the first time. Verified `lrmjump` **22/22** both solvers; fast/slow cargo suites green. |
| [E-521](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-521.md) | openvaf/hir/src/lib.rs, openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, openvaf/parser/src/grammar/items/module.rs, examples/lrmudf_examples/ (new) | **ANALOG USER-DEFINED FUNCTIONS, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 4.7.** A `parameter` declared INSIDE a function body -- a legal analog_function_item and the carrier of 4.7.1's local-shadows-module scoping rule -- CRASHED codegen ('attempted to read undefined value'): parameter reads lower to `ParamKind::Param` slots bound from the module declaration walk, which descends into named blocks but never into FUNCTION scopes, so a function-local parameter's slot was never defined. A read now inlines the parameter's DEFAULT EXPRESSION; module-parameter references inside it resolve to real OSDI slots, which is exactly the clause's rule -- verified from the netlist: overriding a module parameter the function reads propagates in (14 -> 24; through a chained local default 42 -> 82), overriding the SHADOWED one does not, and \$param_given on a local is constantly false. That exposed a LATENT bug: the constant answer was an INT constant where `ParamGiven` is BOOL-typed, so the bool->int cast at an integer-assignment site panicked the MIR folder ('invalid int operation bicast') -- and E-398's paramset arm (`iconst(1)`) had the identical bug waiting; both answer BOOL constants now. **A pure OUTPUT array argument had inout semantics** (4.7.2.3 'initialized, zero (0) if numeric ... the argument passed to it is reset to zero'): the Enhancement-18 whole-array path copied the caller's elements in regardless of direction, so the body read {5,6} where the LRM mandates zeros and an unassigned output array left the caller unchanged where the LRM mandates the reset. Array formals check the direction first now -- input/inout copy in (E-18/E-33 untouched), pure output zero-inits element-wise, and the existing unconditional copy-out becomes exactly the mandated reset (the audit's case reads 100, not 1256). **The LRM's own Example 3 was a parse error**: array formals with the range on the DIRECTION line (`inout [0:1]a; real a[0:1];`) died at the bracket -- while the compiler's own namerange elaboration REWRITES `output o[0:1];` into exactly that form, generating syntax its own parser refused. `func_arg` accepts the range now; the dimensions come from the mandatory data-type declaration (the LRM's own dual-declaration redundancy), Example 3 compiles verbatim and computes 816, and the namerange round-trip is healed. Function-local parameter ARRAYS stay a clean located error. The 4.7.1 restriction list is untouched (recursion, access functions, zero-arg functions all still rejected); named blocks in bodies and constant-context calls stay as DOCUMENTED relaxations; the compliance doc's section cited a nonexistent clause ('LRM 5.11') and glossed over both crashes -- rewritten, and a stale handbook bullet claiming break/continue do not exist (missed by E-520's doc pass) fixed with it. Verified `lrmudf` **15/15** both solvers. |
| [E-522](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-522.md) | openvaf/hir_def/src/{expr.rs,body/lower.rs}, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{validation.rs,validation/body.rs}, openvaf/hir_lower/src/stmt.rs, openvaf/parser/src/grammar/stmts.rs, examples/lrmevents_examples/ (new) | **ANALOG EVENTS, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 5.10 (compiler half).** `cross()` fired during .dc sweeps and OFF THE NEWTON ITERATES of the t=0 operating point -- 5.10.3.2 says it 'will not generate events for non-transient analyses' and 'can only generate an event after the simulation time has advanced from zero'. The FIRED bool is gated on analysis('tran') && \$abstime > 0 now, while the STATE keeps tracking through DC so the first transient step compares against the converged operating point. `above()` is the clause's deliberate contrast (it SHALL fire during initialization and dc sweeps): seeding its previous-value state with the current value made the first evaluation a non-edge BY CONSTRUCTION, so an expression positive from the first iterate never produced the mandated initialization event -- the fired bool ORs in initial_step && expr > 0, and the suite pins above firing mid-sweep next to cross staying silent. **AN UNRECOGNIZED EVENT SILENTLY RAN THE BODY ON EVERY EVALUATION**: any @(...) not literally cross/above/timer/step was DROPPED -- @(absdelta(...)), @(named_event) and a plain typo all became run-always statements (120 executions measured over a 10-point transient). A distinct Event::Invalid variant now draws a targeted error (absdelta cites 5.10.3.4/Annex C.7, identifiers cite 5.10.4) and lowering never runs the body. **PLACEMENT RULES ENFORCED**: nested event controls ('not allowed' -- the nested form lowered as ANDed gates, a silently DEAD statement), cross/above under a runtime if/case arm or inside repeat/while/for (the event state advances only when the branch executes, so detection compares against a stale value; genvar ifs fold and genvar for-loops unroll, so both legal shapes pass), and analog filters inside the event EXPRESSION (the body-side check existed; the expression was validated under the ENCLOSING ctx, so @(cross(ddt(...))) compiled silently). **THE COMMA SEPARATOR** of 5.10.1 works -- and the new suite's check [8] immediately caught the first cut: the parser accepted the comma but the hir_def unit splitter segments on `or` tokens, so comma-joined units MERGED and the second member was dropped; a depth-1 comma now splits units (phase-list commas sit at depth 2, call-argument commas inside the expression node). A NONZERO cross/above tolerance warns it is accepted but not honored (measured 5.6us against a requested 1ns time_tol; 0.0 means 'the simulator shall apply a suitable value', which is exactly what happens and stays silent). Verified `lrmevents` **22/22** both solvers. |
| [E-523](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-523.md) | openvaf/hir_ty/src/{inference,diagnostics}.rs, openvaf/hir_lower/src/{expr.rs,lib.rs}, openvaf/osdi/src/lib.rs, openvaf/test_data/ui/ddx.log, examples/lrmcoreops_examples/ (new) | **THE CORE ANALOG OPERATORS, SQUARED WITH LRM 4.5.5-4.5.7 (compiler half).** `absdelay` without maxdelay now FREEZES its delay: 4.5.7 says 'the value of the delay argument td when the module instance is initialized shall be used', but td was re-evaluated every step, so `absdelay(V(in), 1m + 1m*V(ctl))` drifted its delay mid-transient (measured answering to td=2m where the LRM requires the initial 1m). The `(flow, NATURE_ACCESS_NODES)` signature maps to the same DDX_FLOW lowering now -- forward `ddx(I^2,I)=2I` exact, reversed orientation negated, a flow that is not a system unknown gives exactly 0 -- with the stale help text ('not I(a,b)') rewritten. **idtmod with no ic forced nothing**: 4.5.5 defaults the ic to 0 and says it 'shall force the DC solution', but `idtmod(x)` lowered with NO constraint -- an unconstrained integrator unknown, a singular matrix ngspice regularized to an arbitrary value. The no-ic signature routes through the IdtKind::Ic path with a zero operand now: DC pins to 0 exactly, no singular-matrix warning. Two audited deviations stay documented (idt assert-reset settles via a short first-order decay; ddt/idt tolerance arguments validated then discarded). Verified `lrmcoreops` **18/18** both solvers. |
| [E-524](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-524.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation/body.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation.rs, examples/lrmfilters_examples/ (new) | **THE TRANSITION AND LAPLACE DEFAULTS, SQUARED WITH LRM 4.5.8-4.5.15.** An EXPLICIT zero rise/fall time now takes ``default_transition``: 4.5.8 says 'if neither rise_time nor fall_time are specified OR ARE EQUAL TO ZERO, the rise and fall time default to the value defined by `default_transition' -- but the lowering clamped non-positive times before consulting the directive, so `transition(s, 0.0, 0.0)` under a 1u directive stepped instantaneously while the omitted-argument spelling ramped; each edge time now selects at runtime (the written time if positive, the directive value otherwise; mid-ramp pinned at 0.5). **A bare transition() was NOT a filter**: with no times and no directive it lowered to the raw input -- no smoothing, no state, a discontinuous driver stayed discontinuous. `transition(x)` and `transition(x, td)` route through the rate-limited track machinery with an unbounded requested rate now, which the finite-gain clamp turns into a negligible ~1ns ramp: DC is an exact pass-through, a transient settles within nanoseconds. **Solution-dependent laplace coefficients are audible**: Table 4-20 classes the vectors as CONSTANT arguments frozen at analysis start, but this implementation re-evaluates them every iteration, so a coefficient reading the solution TRACKS -- a useful extension that deviated in silence. A recursive op-dependence walk (potential/flow/temperature/vt/abstime) draws one warning per statement naming the filter; parameter-built coefficients stay silent and none of the thirteen big CMC models trips it. The compliance doc's 4.3 was rewritten around the three approximation blocks (transition amplitude/AC corner, zi bilinear with tau/t0 ignored, laplace tracking) and E-514's over-broad 'Table 4-20 honoured exactly' claim corrected. Verified `lrmfilters` **14/14** both solvers. |
| [E-525](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-525.md) | openvaf/hir/src/elaborate.rs, openvaf/parser/src/grammar/items/module.rs, openvaf/syntax/src/ast/node_ext.rs, examples/lrmhier_examples/ (new), examples/vafinstcheck_examples/ (pin updated) | **HIERARCHY, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 6. FIVE SILENT BUGS.** `defparam` was DROPPED wherever a generate construct existed: the generate-block parser had no defparam arm (and elaboration re-renders the region from the tree, swallowing the parse error -- the E-390 failure shape again), while the module-level rewrite rebuilt the item region from the TYPED item list, which defparam is deliberately not part of, so module-scope defparams beside a generate vanished in the splice. The parser gained defparam/branch/aliasparam arms, the rewrite splices by byte range keeping everything else verbatim, and generate blocks render defparams with genvars folded and per-iteration renames applied -- precedence over #(...) preserved two levels down. **#(.\$mfactor(4)) and .\$xposition(...) child overrides compiled clean and did NOTHING** (the SYSFUN parsed into an ERROR node whose diagnostic existed only on the pre-elaboration tree): the full 6.3.6 multiplicity transform applies as single-token text holes now -- reads compose (multiplicative x netlist m, positions additive), flow-contribution RHS x m, flow probes / m for the per-copy read-back, noise calls / sqrt(m) so contributed-current noise POWER scales x m and contributed-voltage / m, ddx/\$limit probe references exempt, overrides composing down the hierarchy (x2 under x4 = x8, verified to the microamp) -- with duplicate and unknown .\$ overrides as targeted errors. **\$param_given saw no hierarchy overrides**: flattening bakes an instance value in as the new default, so it reported NOT GIVEN and every configuration guard took the default branch; instance-bound and defparam-applied names now rewrite to a true literal, netlist overrides keeping the native OSDI path. **A scalar net on a 2-bit port silently drove both bits** (a broadcast fallback) -- now the 6.5.7.1 error naming both modules; buses, part-selects, concatenations connect as before. **A mixed ordered+named #(...) list bound the named half and dropped the positional values** -- now the Syntax 6-2 error, matching the E-395 port check. Compliance section 6 rewritten with the fixes, the remaining refused-with-diagnostics gaps, and the accepted extensions. Verified `lrmhier` **22/22** both solvers. |
| [E-526](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-526.md) | openvaf/hir/src/elaborate.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation/body.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/validation.rs, examples/lrmcontrib_examples/ (new), examples/vafconstidx_examples/ (extended) | **ANALOG BEHAVIOR AND CONTRIBUTIONS, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 5.** A hierarchical contribution MERGED into the child's branch: 5.6.8.1 creates 'a new unnamed branch ... in the module containing the direct contribution statements' (5.5.4: distinct even when the child already has one between the same nodes), but flattening rewrote `c1.mid` textually, so the parent's `V(p, c1.mid) <+ 0.5` landed on the child's own branch -- the retention rule then DISCARDED the child's flow contribution (warning L022 on fully legal code) and the child's mirror of its own branch flow read the merged potential-source current, 1.0mA where the LRM requires the child's 0.5mA. Each hierarchical target now synthesizes its own named branch over the final node pair; same-module probes of the pair alias onto it (reversed order negated), child NAMED branches keep merging per 5.6.8.2, port members keep their documented resolution error, and the transform composes exactly with E-525's multiplicity machinery. **Guarded indirect assignments were singular matrices**: the validator checked only `<+` against its context rules, so `if (V(pin)>0.5) V(out): V(a,b)==0;` -- and the same inside @(initial_step) -- compiled silently and left the constraint as 0=0 whenever the guard was off. Both are compile errors per 5.6.7/5.6.5 now; the ctx machinery already encodes the constant-expression carve-out, so legal shapes pass. **A direct <+ onto an indirectly-assigned branch was silently absorbed** (the constraint pinned the value; the solver moved the implicit unknown to soak up the contribution): a whole-body scan with orientation-normalized branch identity reports the 5.6.7.2 error naming both statements. **V(in[width-2]) folds**: 5.5.2's index 'must be a constant expression', which INCLUDES parameters; a new elaboration pass folds vectored-NET bit-selects and freezes the index's TRANSITIVE parameter support structural (derived localparams freeze the parameter underneath), so a card override is refused with the standard warning, never silently ignored -- array-variable indices untouched. The clause's deliberate relaxations (contributions in runtime loops, do-while, generalized indirect LHS, out-of-scope named-block writes, the L017 both-natures read) are disclosed in compliance section 5. Verified `lrmcontrib` **19/19** and the extended `vafconstidx` **27/27**, both solvers. |
| [E-527](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-527.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/expr.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{validation.rs,validation/body.rs,inference.rs}, examples/lrmkernel_examples/ (new), examples/{table_model,alias,deckdomain,dropguard,lrmfuncs,rangeguard}_examples/ (pins updated) | **THE KERNEL AND RANDOM SYSTEM FUNCTIONS, AUDITED AGAINST LRM 9 (compiler half).** \$bound_step honors the SMALLEST active bound (9.17.2) -- the lowering overwrote the place per call, so several calls in one evaluation left the LAST as the cap; it takes the minimum now, with E-24's negative discontinuity sentinel still winning. **\$table_model is brought to the whole of clause 9.21**: default LINEAR extrapolation on both ends per Tables 9-31/9-32 (it clamped unless an 'L' appeared -- the audit's y=2x table reads 8.0 above and 0.0 below, was 6.0/2.0); per-DIMENSION comma-separated control sub-strings, outermost first (any code used to apply to every axis -- '1C,1L' on the 2-D grid now clamps y and extends x); up to two extrapolation characters set each END; closest-point 'D' with 9.21.4's farther-from-zero tie rule; 'E' errors on extrapolation at run time; the ';N' dependent-column selector is honoured (validation parsed it, lowering ignored it); the normative 9.21.1 N+M-column isoline files read -- RAGGED isolines included, via a recursive isoline tree, with the self-describing grid format kept as an exact-token-count-gated extension -- and the 1-D '{xs}, '{ys} array pair joins the interleaved layout; '2'/'I' stay located refusals, and the runtime array form keeps 1/3 with same-both-ends C/L honestly. **The 9.13.2 domain rules fire on the deck route**: 'mean, degree_of_freedom, and k_stage shall be greater than zero. Otherwise an error shall be reported' -- a deck-supplied violation aborts with the mandated runtime error for all five listed distributions, both families (chi-square/t/erlang used to hand the RNG the raw value and return deviates outside their own support; exponential/poisson clamped in silence). **No-default \$simprobe is the 9.16 error** (compile warning + runtime fatal; the default form keeps returning its default -- the actual LRM fallback); the alias builtins are analog-initial-only per 9.20; type_string warns outside a paramset. **\$vt moved to the 2019 exact SI k and q**, equal to `P_K*T/`P_Q exactly under PHYSICAL_CONSTANTS_NIST2018 (the ~1ppm default-set difference is documented), and the stale lower_rng comment claiming a seed advance that does not exist was rewritten to the pure-(seed, salt) contract. Verified `lrmkernel` **43/43** both solvers. |
| [E-528](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-528.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/callbacks.rs, openvaf/sim_back/src/{topology.rs,noise.rs,dae/builder.rs}, openvaf/osdi/src/{metadata.rs,lib.rs}, openvaf/test_data/{osdi/noise.snap,contributions/*.snap,dae/diode_va_system.snap}, examples/lrmnoise_examples/ (new), examples/noisecorr_examples/ (rewritten to the LRM semantics), examples/noisejw_examples/ (fixture) | **NOISE CORRELATION FOLLOWS THE CALL, NOT THE LABEL (compiler half of LRM 4.6.4).** 4.6.4.6: 'perfectly correlated noise is generated by using the output of ONE noise function for more than one noise source' -- and 4.6.4.1 makes the name a REPORTING label whose contributions are 'combined in the noise contribution summary'. E-42's coherent-amplitude machinery was keyed on the name string, so two SEPARATE calls sharing a label were treated as one physical source: V(a,b) <+ white_noise(p,'w'); V(b,c) <+ -white_noise(p,'w'); cancelled to an exact 0 where the LRM requires the uncorrelated sqrt(2) power sum. The correlation key is now the CALL SITE: hir_lower's per-call index (which already existed precisely to keep white_noise(x)-white_noise(x) nonzero through optimization) threads through the topology extraction and the DAE into the OSDI source name after a \x1f separator. One call's output routed into several contributions -- the shared-output idiom, anti-phase cancellation, scaled uses, ddt()-shaped waves -- keeps summing coherently (ncorrA still 0 exactly); two same-labelled calls read 1.414e-6 (were 0); the label still combines in the summary. One OSDI snapshot and five topology/dae dumps updated; every diff is exactly the new idx field and name suffixes. noise_table's parameter-fed vector/filename forms (4.6.4.3) are disclosed as located refusals, and the compliance doc's ac_stim example phase is finally in radians (`M_PI/2, not 90.0). Verified `lrmnoise` **17/17** and the rewritten `noisecorr` both solvers. |
| [E-530](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-530.md) | openvaf/sim_back/src/module_info.rs, openvaf/osdi/src/{metadata.rs,lib.rs}, examples/osdimc_examples/ (new) | **VERILOG-A PARAMETERS DECLARE THEIR OWN MONTE-CARLO STATISTICS (compiler half).** `(* std=<sigma> *)` on a parameter declares an absolute standard deviation, `(* std_rel=<fraction> *)` one relative to the resolved nominal, and `(* dist="gauss"\|"uniform" *)` the distribution (gauss default; for uniform the value is the HALF-WIDTH -- 'sigma of a uniform' is ambiguous, so the manual says which). LRM 2.9 attributes are the sanctioned vendor vehicle and the conventions match the ecosystem's own: quantities are numeric literals, enumerations are strings, exactly like `type="instance"`. The values ride a new **OSDI_STAT_PARAM_{COUNTS,INFOS} side-table** -- the absdelay/terminal-short mechanism -- as 16-byte {param_id, dist_flags, std} records whose ids mirror param_opvar's chained ordering exactly; the descriptor struct is UNTOUCHED, so an old object simply lacks the symbols and an object with them loads in an old simulator unchanged (after E-529's audit of what stride drift costs, that was the entire design constraint). Six diagnostics guard the surface: a negative or non-literal sigma (the string spelling `std="25.0"` included) and `std` beside `std_rel` are located ERRORS; an unknown distribution, statistics on a non-real parameter or a localparam, and `dist` without a sigma are located WARNINGS. Statistics compose with `type="instance"`: instance = per-device mismatch, model = per-card process, nothing new invented. Verified `osdimc` **24/24** both solvers; 13 CMC models compile zero-warning; cargo fast+slow green. |
| [E-531](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-531.md) | openvaf/hir_lower/src/stmt.rs, openvaf/parser/src/grammar/items/module.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{validation.rs,validation/body.rs}, openvaf/sim_back/src/module_info.rs, examples/huntfix_examples/ (new) | **A NOISE LINE SILENTLY KILLED BSIM4 (the bug-hunt round, compiler half).** The compiled bsim4.va -- 12.6k lines, compiles zero-warning, runs every analysis cleanly -- conducted EXACTLY ZERO at every bias. The charge model was alive (AC showed the real ~15 fF gate capacitance); the channel was dead: the model computed a healthy Ids = 3.8 mA and then cdrain = Ids*Vdseff with Vdseff = 0, because the INTERNAL drain never followed the terminal. The `V(d,di) <+ 0.0` collapse of the default rdsMod=0 path existed in the source and in the runtime's own arithmetic -- but not in the compiled topology: hir_lower defined the branch's IsVoltageSrc place on EVERY contribution, last-write-wins, and the access-region noise `I(di,d) <+ white_noise(...)` -- reversed spelling, lowered AFTER the conditional voltage contribution -- reclassified the branch as never-a-voltage-source. LRM 4.6.4 makes noise ZERO in every large-signal analysis: a noise-only right-hand side (a noise call, possibly negated/scaled/summed with other noise calls) carries no source kind, and the lowering now preserves the branch's classification across them (a branch whose ONLY contributions are noise classifies exactly as before). Stock BSIM4 conducts (-1.27 mA at Vgs=Vds=1 V, clean Vg family, rdsmod=1 unchanged); PSP103/BSIMBULK/BSIMSOI bit-identical; four test snapshots moved by pure SSA renumbering only. TWO LESSONS RECORDED: the corpus only COMPILED bsim4 -- nothing ever simulated it, which is how a dead flagship model stayed invisible -- and the compactmodels suite had PINNED the zero as a 'known finding', so the fix announced itself by flipping that check into health. ALSO: attributes on analog-function items PARSE (the attr recovery set contained bare IDENT, so `(* desc *)`/`(* std *)` on a function item died as "unexpected token '*)'"); statistics attributes on a NON-parameter warn (the shadow-variable typo ran a Monte-Carlo that varied nothing); a duplicated table-file abscissa draws a warning naming the file and knot. Verified `huntfix` **12/12** both solvers incl. stock BSIM4 conduction; cargo fast+slow green; 13 CMC models zero-warning. |
| [E-532](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-532.md) | openvaf/basedb/src/lints.rs, openvaf/hir/src/body.rs, openvaf/hir_ty/src/{validation.rs,validation/body.rs}, openvaf/sim_back/src/diagnostics.rs, examples/huntfix2_examples/ (new), examples/rangeguard_examples/ (3 fixtures corrected) | **THE OPENVAF-R HUNT ROUND (compiler half): a default off its own range now speaks, and a retraction is pinned.** A PARAMETER DEFAULT THAT VIOLATES ITS OWN RANGE WAS SILENT: `parameter real r = -1.0 from (0:inf);` compiled zero-warning and SIMULATED with r = -1 -- a negative resistance from a declaration whose whole purpose is to forbid it -- while the same value in `.model` is refused at setup ("out of bounds"). The range machinery only ever judged GIVEN values; the author's own default was exempt per the CMC must-give convention the handbook documents, but the exemption said nothing. The new `param_default_out_of_range` lint (L027, warn by default) judges a CONSTANT default against CONSTANT `from`/`exclude` constraints at compile time, mirroring the generated check_param exactly: a `from` list is a union (any match accepts), `exclude` is absolute (one foldable hit reports regardless of the rest), any non-constant `from` member makes the union unjudgeable and nothing is said. The `inf` bound literal now const-folds -- without it `from (0:inf)`, the most common spelling of all, was unjudgeable. The deliberate must-give idiom keeps its silence per declaration via `(* openvaf_allow="param_default_out_of_range" *)`; 26/26 bundled industry models produce ZERO hits. Three rangeguard fixtures turned out to carry genuinely illegal defaults (they probed the exclude-cover checker with arbitrary values); two of them -- shapes with NO legal value at all -- now pin that L027 speaks exactly where the cover checker deliberately stays silent. THE DISCARDED-CONTRIBUTION REPORT LEARNED "NOISE-ONLY": the hunt logged "opposite-kind noise on a classified branch is silently dropped" and was wrong about the silently -- E-400's warning already fired; the hunt's own probe had swallowed the compiler's stderr (recorded in the ledger). The real gap was the message: since E-531 a noise-only contribution never decides a branch's kind, so "the last contribution decides" was exactly wrong for the very site the report pointed at. Contribution sites now carry a `noise_only` classification (the HIR mirror of the lowering's detector) and the report states that the noise-only site never decides the branch kind but its NOISE vanishes with the losing kind. THE RETRACTION, PINNED: the hunt claimed same-named `white_noise` calls must sum coherently (LRM correlation-by-name) and measured 2S where it expected 4S. The claim was WRONG -- the project's own LRM audit (4.6.4.6, p.92) and E-528 established labels-group-reporting / correlation-follows-the-call, and the shared-output idiom measures EXACT (contributed twice: 4S to every digit; anti-series: exact cancellation; scaled by 2: power x4). "Fixing" the finding would have regressed an audited fix; three closed-form suite checks now pin all three shapes so the semantics cannot drift toward the bug OR the retracted fix. AND LIMEXP'S KNEE IS WRITTEN DOWN: handbook 4.4 called the stateless limexp "exact in every analysis"; above the knee at ln(1e30) ~= 69.08 that is false (a junction forced to x ~= 193 converges to the tangent value, tens of decades below true exp). The entry now states the knee, the tangent form and the divergence, and two suite checks MEASURE the knee so the documentation cannot drift from the implementation. Verified `huntfix2` **21/21** and `rangeguard` **75/75** both solvers; cargo fast+slow green, zero snapshot churn; full sweep **446/446** ALL OK. |
